		private void grid_RowDoubleClickCommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string text = ".!" + enumerator.Current;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdAdd_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = default(string);
			string text3 = default(string);
			bool? flag = ((MMViewModel)this).ShowAddChildDialog(text, ref text2, ref text3, false, true);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				string text4 = ".!" + text2;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text4, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = parameter as string;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).RemoveAllVariablesFromBatchProcessPlot(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main." + text));
				((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main"), (IEnumerable<string>)new Collection<string> { text }, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdDuplicate_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main", "child_count"));
			if (num.HasValue)
			{
				_ = num.Value;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).NotifyUpdates = false;
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.SuspendNotifications();
			string arg = default(string);
			((MMViewModel)this).ShowAddChildDialog(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main"), ref arg, true);
			if (grdChild_GridData is IList list && SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1 && list.Count > SelectedRowIndexes[0] && list[SelectedRowIndexes[0]] is grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate)
			{
				grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId();
				string title = grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetTitle();
				string variables = grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetVariables();
				string description = grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetDescription();
				string plotType = grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetPlotType();
				string xMLString = grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetXMLString();
				Collection<string> collection = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main", "child_ids"));
				string text = null;
				int num2 = 0;
				do
				{
					text = CreateNewVariableName(grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId(), $"DP{++num2}");
				}
				while (collection.Contains(text));
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.{arg}", "id", (object)text);
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.{text}.Input.TITLE", "value", (object)title);
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.{text}.Input.PLOTVARS", "value", (object)variables);
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.{text}.Input.DESCRIPTION", "value", (object)description);
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.{text}.Input.TYPE", "value", (object)plotType);
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.{text}.Input.PLOTXML", "value", (object)xMLString);
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).NotifyUpdates = true;
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.ResumeNotifications();
			((MMViewModel)this).UpdateDisplay((object)null);
			((MMViewModel)this).UpdateAll();
		}

		private string CreateNewVariableName(string name, string suffix)
		{
			if (name.Length < 8 - suffix.Length)
			{
				return name + suffix;
			}
			return name.Substring(0, 8 - suffix.Length) + suffix;
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetSelectedIds()
		{
			IList gridData = grdChild_GridData as IList;
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null)
			{
				foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
				{
					list.Add(selectedRowIndex);
				}
			}
			foreach (int item in list)
			{
				if (item >= 0 && item < gridData.Count)
				{
					grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate = gridData[item] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate;
					yield return grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				chkTotBalVisibility = ((!(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("TBal_ChkBox")) == "1")) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
				chkCompBalVisibility = ((!(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("CBal_ChkBox")) == "1")) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
				SetchkReactionVisibility();
			}
		}

		private void SetchkReactionVisibility()
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main", "type"));
			if (text != null && _blockType.Contains(text.ToLower()))
			{
				chkReactionVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		public class MMGeneric_Hcur2D_Result2_LabelConverter : IValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return value.ToString() switch
				{
					"DUTY" => Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_Heat_duty, 
					"PRES" => Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_Pressure, 
					"TEMP" => Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_Temperature, 
					"VFRAC" => Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_Vapor_fraction, 
					_ => value, 
				};
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		public class MMGeneric_Hcurve_Result2_LabelConverter : IValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				string text = value.ToString();
				switch (text)
				{
				case "DUTY":
					return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_Heat_duty;
				case "TEMP":
					return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_Temperature;
				case "PRES":
					return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_Pressure;
				case "VFRAC":
					return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_Vapor_fraction;
				case "TOTAL TEMP":
					return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_TOTAL_TEMP;
				case "TOTAL PRES":
					return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_TOTAL_PRES;
				default:
					if (text.StartsWith("VAPOR MOLEFLOW"))
					{
						return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_VAPOR_MOLEFLOW + text.Substring(14);
					}
					if (text.StartsWith("LIQUID MOLEFLOW"))
					{
						return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_LIQUID_MOLEFLOW + text.Substring(15);
					}
					if (text.StartsWith("VAPOR MOLEFRAC"))
					{
						return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_VAPOR_MOLEFRAC + text.Substring(14);
					}
					if (text.StartsWith("LIQUID MOLEFRAC"))
					{
						return Resources.MMMHeatx_Hcurve_Result_ViewModel_LIQUID_MOLEFRAC + text.Substring(15);
					}
					if (text.StartsWith("TOTAL MASSFLOW") && text.Length >= 14)
					{
						return Resources.TOTAL_MASSFLOW + text.Substring(14);
					}
					return value;
				}
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("_123Res3", (object)"VFRAC_OUT2");
					break;
				case "OPT_RANGE3":
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("_123Res1", (object)"TEMP_OUT3");
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("_123Res2", (object)"DUTY_OUT3");
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("_123Res3", (object)"VFRAC_OUT3");
					break;
				case "OPT_RANGE4":
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("_123Res1", (object)"TEMP_OUT4");
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("_123Res2", (object)"DUTY_OUT4");
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("_123Res3", (object)"VFRAC_OUT4");
					break;
				}
			}
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("_123PropRes") == null)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("_123PropRes", ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("_123Results"));
			}
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
			if (pd != lbtuPdrop_var1_Property || !(subPropertyName == "Value") || !raiseValueChanged)
			{
				return;
			}
			object obj = lbtuPdrop_var1;
			CxvVariableData val = (CxvVariableData)((obj is CxvVariableData) ? obj : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string units = val.Units;
			string strB = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.@_123Presdrop.@id", "uom"));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(units) && string.Compare(units, strB, StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0)
			{
				raiseValueChanged = false;
				try
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.@_123Presdrop.@id", "uom", (object)units);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				raiseValueChanged = true;
			}
		}

		}

		private void cmdAspenProcessManual_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).Parent.GetType().Namespace;
			if (text.Contains("Blocks"))
			{
				int num = text.LastIndexOf(".");
				string text2 = text.Substring(num + 1);
				text2 = text2.ToUpper();
				string text3;
				switch (text2)
				{
				case "HYCYC":

		private string RecType
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_recType))
				{
					string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").Replace("Top.appModelV8.", string.Empty);
					if (text.StartsWith("Reactions.Reactions"))
					{
						_recType = "reactions";
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("Model Analysis Tools"))
					{
						if (text.StartsWith("Model Analysis Tools.Data-Fit.Data-Set"))
						{
							_recType = "data_set";
						}
						else
						{
							_recType = "model_analysis";
						}
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("Properties.Analysis"))
					{
						_recType = "analysis";
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("Convergence.Convergence"))
					{
						_recType = "convergence";
					}
					else if (text.StartsWith("Properties.Data"))
					{
						_recType = "data";
					}
				}
				return _recType;
			}
		}

		private string GetLocalizedCol1(string key, string defaultOptionValue)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
			{
				string name = Regex.Replace(key, "[^a-zA-Z0-9]", "_").ToLower();
				text = ApwnRecTypeResource.ResourceManager.GetObject(name, ApwnRecTypeResource.Culture) as string;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			return defaultOptionValue;
		}

		public bool CanPaste
		{
			get
			{
				if (!ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("Aspen Plus Selection"))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("Aspen Plus Selection Type"))
				{
					IDataObject dataObject = ClipboardWrapper.GetDataObject();
					if (dataObject != null && dataObject.GetData("Aspen Plus Selection Type", autoConvert: false) is MemoryStream stream)
					{
						string text = new StreamReader(stream).ReadToEnd();
						string text2 = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
						try
						{
							return CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text2, "action_status", "CanPaste:" + text)).CanInvoke;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public CxvLogicalPropertyData cmdPaste_CommandCanExecute
		{
			get
			{
				object value = ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(cmdPaste_CommandCanExecuteProperty);
				return (CxvLogicalPropertyData)((value is CxvLogicalPropertyData) ? value : null);
			}
		}

		public int AllSelectedRecordsCount
		{
			get
			{
				return _allSelectedRecordsCount;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _allSelectedRecordsCount)
				{
					_allSelectedRecordsCount = value;
					OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CanPasteChanged;

		public event EventHandler AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged;

		private void cmdAdd_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = null;
			bool? flag = ((MMViewModel)this).ShowAddChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				string path = ".!" + text2;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void cmdEdit_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string current = enumerator.Current;
				string path = ".!" + current;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
		}

		private void cmdCopy_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).CopyChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection);
			OnCanPasteChanged();
		}

		private void cmdRename_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			int allSelectedRecordsCount = AllSelectedRecordsCount;
			if (allSelectedRecordsCount != 0 && allSelectedRecordsCount <= 1)
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
				string text2 = GetSelectedIds().First();
				((MMViewModel)this).ShowRenameChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdHide_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).HideNodeChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
		}

		private void cmdReveal_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).ShowRevealNodeDialog(text);
		}

		private void OnCanPasteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanPasteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanPasteChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CanPaste");
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).DoPaste(text);
		}

		private void grid_RowDoubleClickCommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string path = ".!" + enumerator.Current;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged()
		{
			if (this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged != null)
			{
				this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("AllSelectedRecordsCount");
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetSelectedIds()
		{
			IList gridData = grdChild_GridData as IList;
			foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
			{
				grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate = gridData[selectedRowIndex] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate;
				yield return grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		private void GoToNodeEx(string path)
		{
			string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
			text = ((!text.Contains("Blocks")) ? text.Replace("Top.appModelV8.", "") : text.Replace("Data.", "").Substring(text.IndexOf("Blocks")));
			if (text.EndsWith("Design-Spec"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Design-Spec", ApwnResources.BrowserDesignSpec), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith(".Sensitivity"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Model Analysis Tools", ApwnResources.BrowserModelAnalysisTools).Replace("Sensitivity", ApwnResources.BrowserSensitivity), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith("Data-Set"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Data-Fit", ApwnResources.BrowserDataFit).Replace("Data-Set", ApwnResources.BrowserDataSet), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith("Generation"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Properties!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("PetroCharacterization", ApwnResources.BrowserPetroCharacterization).Replace("Generation", ApwnResources.BrowserGeneration), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith("Data-Fit"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Data-Fit", ApwnResources.BrowserDataFit), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(path, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedRowFromScript(int row)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes == null)
			{
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>();
			}
			SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
			SelectedRowIndexes.Add(row);
			AllSelectedRecordsCount = 1;
		}

		private void MMGeneric_List1D_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			dynamic ancestorFromType = Utilities.GetAncestorFromType((DependencyObject)(object)this, typeof(ContentControl), true);
			dynamic val = ancestorFromType.Header;
			if (val.ToString().Equals(ApwnResources.BrowserCustomForms))
			{
				((UIElement)(object)cmdHide).Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				((UIElement)(object)cmdReveal).Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

			private bool GetIsBlockActive(string id)
			{
				int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main." + id, "status_code"));
				if (num.HasValue && (num.Value & 0x2000) != 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public string GetId()
			{

			}

			{
				if (pd.Name == "chkActive_var1" && subPropertyName == "Value")
				{
					bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "chkActive_var1.Value"));
					if (flag.HasValue)
					{
						IsBlockActive = flag.Value;
					}
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
			}

			protected override void OnUpdateData()
			{
				if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
				{
					Collection<string> collection = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main", "child_ids"));
					if (collection != null)
					{
						foreach (string item in collection)
						{
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_Active." + item, (object)GetIsBlockActive(item));
						}
					}
				}
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_Active." + text, (object)IsBlockActive);
					((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("chkActive_var1");
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
			}

			get
			{
				object value = ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(cmdRevealCanExecuteProperty);
				return (CxvLogicalPropertyData)((value is CxvLogicalPropertyData) ? value : null);
			}
		}

		public ICommand cmdPaste_Command
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdPaste_Command == null)
				{
					_cmdPaste_Command = (ICommand)new CxvDelegateCommand<object>((Action<object>)cmdPaste_CommandExecute, (object)this, cmdPaste_CommandCanExecuteProperty.Name + ".Value");
				}
				return _cmdPaste_Command;
			}
		}

		public bool CanPaste
		{
			get
			{
				if (!ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("Aspen Plus Selection"))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("Aspen Plus Selection Type"))
				{
					IDataObject dataObject = ClipboardWrapper.GetDataObject();
					if (dataObject != null && dataObject.GetData("Aspen Plus Selection Type", autoConvert: false) is MemoryStream stream)
					{
						string text = new StreamReader(stream).ReadToEnd();
						string text2 = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
						try
						{
							return CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text2, "action_status", "CanPaste:" + text)).CanInvoke;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public CxvLogicalPropertyData cmdPaste_CommandCanExecute
		{
			get
			{
				object value = ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(cmdPaste_CommandCanExecuteProperty);
				return (CxvLogicalPropertyData)((value is CxvLogicalPropertyData) ? value : null);
			}
		}

		public int AllSelectedRecordsCount
		{
			get
			{
				return _allSelectedRecordsCount;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _allSelectedRecordsCount)
				{
					_allSelectedRecordsCount = value;
					OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<int> SelectedRowIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				return _SelectedRowIndexes;
			}
			set
			{
				_SelectedRowIndexes = value;
			}
		}

		public object grdChild_GridData => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(grdChild_GridData_Property);

		public event EventHandler CanPasteChanged;

		public event EventHandler AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged;

		private void cmdAdd_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = null;
			bool? flag = ((MMViewModel)this).ShowAddChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				string path = ".!" + text2;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void cmdEdit_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string current = enumerator.Current;
				string path = ".!" + current;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
		}

		private void cmdCopy_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).CopyChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection);
			OnCanPasteChanged();
		}

		private void cmdRename_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			int allSelectedRecordsCount = AllSelectedRecordsCount;
			if (allSelectedRecordsCount != 0 && allSelectedRecordsCount <= 1)
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
				string text2 = GetSelectedIds().First();
				((MMViewModel)this).ShowRenameChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdHide_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).HideNodeChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
		}

		private void cmdReveal_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).ShowRevealNodeDialog(text);
		}

		private void OnCanPasteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanPasteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanPasteChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CanPaste");
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).DoPaste(text);
		}

		private void grid_RowDoubleClickCommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string path = ".!" + enumerator.Current;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged()
		{
			if (this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged != null)
			{
				this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("AllSelectedRecordsCount");
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetSelectedIds()
		{
			IList gridData = grdChild_GridData as IList;
			foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
			{
				grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate = gridData[selectedRowIndex] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate;
				yield return grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				if (((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").EndsWith(".Measurement"))
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("descvar", (object)"NAME");
				}
				else
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("descvar", (object)"DESCRIPTION");
				}
			}
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		private void GoToNodeEx(string path)
		{
			string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
			text = ((!text.Contains("Blocks")) ? text.Replace("Top.appModelV8.", "") : text.Replace("Data.", "").Substring(text.IndexOf("Blocks")));
			if (text.EndsWith("Design-Spec"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Design-Spec", ApwnResources.BrowserDesignSpec), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith(".Sensitivity"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Model Analysis Tools", ApwnResources.BrowserModelAnalysisTools).Replace("Sensitivity", ApwnResources.BrowserSensitivity), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith("Data-Set"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Data-Fit", ApwnResources.BrowserDataFit).Replace("Data-Set", ApwnResources.BrowserDataSet), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith("Generation"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Properties!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("PetroCharacterization", ApwnResources.BrowserPetroCharacterization).Replace("Generation", ApwnResources.BrowserGeneration), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith("Data-Fit"))
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Data-Fit", ApwnResources.BrowserDataFit), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(path, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedRowFromScript(int row)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes == null)
			{
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>();
			}
			SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
			SelectedRowIndexes.Add(row);
			AllSelectedRecordsCount = 1;
		}

					}
					else
					{
						_changingArgs = null;
					}
				}

				public void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
				{
					if (IsRenaming(pd, subPropertyName) && _changingArgs != null)
					{
						object txtName_var = _parentItem.txtName_var1;
						CxvVariableData val = (CxvVariableData)((txtName_var is CxvVariableData) ? txtName_var : null);
						if (val != null)
						{
							string text = _changingArgs.OldValue as string;
							string text2 = val.Value as string;
							if (!string.Equals(text, text2))
							{
								ObjectRenamedAnywhereEventArgs e = ObjectRenamedAnywhereEventArgs.Create(val.DataPath, text, text2);
								if (e != null)
								{
									IUnityContainer unityContainer = ((CxvViewModel)_parentItem).UnityContainer;
									if (unityContainer != null)
									{
										IEventAggregator obj = CxvUnityContainerExtensions.TryResolve<IEventAggregator>(unityContainer);
										if (obj != null)
										{
											((CompositePresentationEvent<ObjectRenamedAnywhereEventArgs>)(object)obj.GetEvent<ObjectRenamedAnywhereEvent>()).Publish(e);
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					_changingArgs = null;
				}

				private static bool IsRenaming(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
				{
					if (pd == txtName_var1_Property)
					{
						return string.Equals(subPropertyName, "Value", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase);
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			internal string RecType
			{
				get
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_recType))
					{
						string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").Replace("Top.appModelV8.", string.Empty);
						int num = text.LastIndexOf(".Data.");
						if (num > 0)
						{
							text = text.Substring(num + 6);
						}
						switch (text)
						{
						case "Reactions.Reactions":
							_recType = "reactions";
							break;
						case "Properties.Analysis":
							_recType = "analysis";
							break;
						case "Convergence.Convergence":
							_recType = "convergence";
							break;
						case "Model Analysis Tools.Data-Fit.Data-Set":
							_recType = "data_set";
							break;
						case "Properties.Data":
							_recType = "data";
							break;
						case "Flowsheeting Options.Calculator":
						{
							int? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.@y.Input.METHOD", "value"));
							if (num2.HasValue && num2.Value == 1)
							{
								_recType = "Excel";
							}
							else if (num2.HasValue && num2.Value == 2)
							{
								_recType = "NeuralNetwork";
							}
							else if (num2.HasValue && num2.Value == 3)
							{
								_recType = "Spreadsheet";
							}
							else
							{
								_recType = "Fortran";
							}
							break;
						}
						}
					}
					return _recType;
				}
			}

			public object txtType_VarCol1
			{
				get
				{
					//IL_0060: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
					//IL_0065: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
					//IL_006c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

					object value = ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(txtType_VarCol1_Property);

						return (object)new CxvManualVariableData

			public bool CanNotBlockHideOrReveal
			{
				get
				{
					string id = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));
					if (!CanBlockHide(id))
					{
						return !CanBlockReveal(id);
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool CanDelete
			{
				get
				{
					string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
					if (text.EndsWith("Units-Sets"))
					{
						switch (CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y")))
						{
						case "ENG":
						case "MET":
						case "SI":
							return false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (text.EndsWith("Parameters.Binary Interaction") || text.EndsWith(".Molecular Structure"))
						{
							return false;
						}
						if (text.EndsWith("Convergence.Convergence"))
						{
							if (CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, "action_status", "DeleteChild")).CanInvoke)
							{
								return true;
							}
							return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.@y"), "can_delete", (string)null)) ?? false;
						}
					}
					try
					{
						string text2 = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.@y");
						CxvActionResult val = ((CxvViewModel)this).InvokeAction("Top.appModelV8.Custom Actions", "HappNode", false, new object[1] { text2 });
						if (val.Succeeded && val.Result is IHNode iHNode)
						{
							dynamic val2 = iHNode.get_AttributeValue((short)31, Type.Missing);
							if (val2 != null && val2 != 0)
							{
								return true;
							}
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						return false;
					}
					return true;
				}
			}

			private string GetLocalizedCol1(string key, string defaultOptionValue)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
				{
					string name = Regex.Replace(key, "[^a-zA-Z0-9]", "_").ToLower();
					text = ApwnRecTypeResource.ResourceManager.GetObject(name, ApwnRecTypeResource.Culture) as string;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return text;
				}
				return defaultOptionValue;
			}

			private bool GetIsBlockActive(string id)
			{
				int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main." + id, "status_code"));
				if (num.HasValue && (num.Value & 0x2000) != 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}

			internal bool CanBlockHide(string id)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main." + id);
				if (RecType.Equals("Excel", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || RecType.Equals("Spreadsheet", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return false;
				}
				CxvActionResult val = ((CxvViewModel)this).InvokeAction("Top.appModelV8.Custom Actions", "HappNode", false, new object[1] { text });
				if (val.Succeeded && val.Result is IHNode iHNode)
				{
					dynamic val2 = iHNode.get_AttributeValue((short)33, Type.Missing);
					if (val2 != null && val2 != 0)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}

			private bool CanBlockReveal(string id)
			{
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main." + id);
				CxvActionResult val = ((CxvViewModel)this).InvokeAction("Top.appModelV8.Custom Actions", "HappNode", false, new object[1] { text });
				if (val.Succeeded && val.Result is IHNode iHNode)
				{
					dynamic val2 = iHNode.get_AttributeValue((short)34, Type.Missing);
					if (val2 != null && val2 != 0)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}

			protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanging(SubPropertyDataChangingEventArgs args)
			{
				if (!_isRenaming && args.Property.Name == "txtName_var1" && args.SubPropertyName == "Value" && !string.Equals(args.OldValue as string, args.NewValue as string, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					_isRenaming = true;
					string text = args.NewValue as string;
					if (((MMViewModel)this).ShowRenameChildDialog(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main"), ref text, true) != true)
					{
						args.Cancel = true;
						_isRenaming = false;
						return;
					}
					_isRenaming = false;
				}
				ObjectRenaming.OnSubPropertyDataChanging(args);
				((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanging(args);
			}

			protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
			{
				if (pd == txtName_var1_Property && subPropertyName == "Value" && txtName_var1 != null)
				{
					object obj = txtName_var1;
					CxvPropertyBase property = ((CxvPropertyData)((obj is CxvCustomTooltipData) ? obj : null)).Property;
					CxvCustomTooltipProperty val = (CxvCustomTooltipProperty)(object)((property is CxvCustomTooltipProperty) ? property : null);
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").Replace("Top.appModelV8.", string.Empty) == "Reactions.Reactions")
					{
						val.ObjectType = "Reaction";
					}
				}
				else if (pd.Name == "chkEnabled_var1" && subPropertyName == "Value")
				{
					object obj2 = chkEnabled_var1;
					CxvParameterPropertyData val2 = (CxvParameterPropertyData)((obj2 is CxvParameterPropertyData) ? obj2 : null);
					if (val2 != null && (CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(val2.Value) ?? false))
					{
						string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
						string item = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));
						((MMViewModel)this).HideNodeChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)new Collection<string> { item }, true);
					}
				}
				ObjectRenaming.OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
				((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
			}

			protected override void OnUpdateData()
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"))))
				{
					string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.@y");
					((CxvViewModel)this).InvokeAction("Top.appModelV8.Custom Actions", "HappNode", false, new object[1] { text });
					if ((CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "chkActive_var1.Value")) ?? false) != IsBlockActive)
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("chkActive_var1.Value", (object)IsBlockActive);
						((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("chkActive_var1");
					}
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").EndsWith(".Calculator"))
					{
						_recType = null;
						((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("txtType_VarCol1");
					}
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
			}

		public bool CanCopy
		{
			get
			{
				if (AllSelectedRecordsCount < 1)
				{
					return false;
				}
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
				if (text.EndsWith(".Molecular Structure") || text.EndsWith(".CO-Package") || text.EndsWith(".Binary Interaction") || text.EndsWith(".Electrolyte Pair") || text.EndsWith(".Electrolyte Tenary") || text.EndsWith(".UNIFAC Groups Binary"))
				{
					return false;
				}
				try
				{
					if (CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, "action_status", "CopyChildren")).CanInvoke)
					{
						return true;
					}
					foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
					{
						if (!(CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(CxsPath.CombinePaths(text, selectedId), "can_copy", (string)null)) ?? false))
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool CanRename
		{
			get
			{
				if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
				{
					return false;
				}
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
				try
				{
					if (CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, "action_status", "RenameChild")).CanInvoke)
					{
						return true;
					}
					foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
					{
						if (!(CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(CxsPath.CombinePaths(text, selectedId), "can_enter_id", (string)null)) ?? false))
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public Visibility RevealVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
				int num = text.LastIndexOf(".");
				string item = text.Substring(num + 1);
				if (HasHideFieldObjects.Contains(item))
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		public Visibility RevealVisibility2
		{
			get
			{
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
				int num = text.LastIndexOf(".");
				string item = text.Substring(num + 1);
				if (HasHideFieldObjects.Contains(item))
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		public bool CanPaste
		{
			get
			{
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
				if (text.EndsWith(".Molecular Structure") || text.EndsWith(".CO-Package") || text.EndsWith(".Binary Interaction") || text.EndsWith(".Electrolyte Pair") || text.EndsWith(".Electrolyte Tenary"))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("Aspen Plus Selection"))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("Aspen Plus Selection Type"))
				{
					IDataObject dataObject = ClipboardWrapper.GetDataObject();
					if (dataObject != null && dataObject.GetData("Aspen Plus Selection Type", autoConvert: false) is MemoryStream stream)
					{
						string text2 = new StreamReader(stream).ReadToEnd();
						try
						{
							return CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, "action_status", "CanPaste:" + text2)).CanInvoke;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool CanObjectsExport
		{
			get
			{
				if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").EndsWith(".Controllers"))
				{
					return false;
				}
				int num = SelectedRowIndexes[0];
				IList list = grdChild_GridData as IList;
				if (num >= 0 && num < list.Count && list[num] is grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate && string.Compare(grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.RecType, "Spreadsheet", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
				{
					string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main." + selectedId);
					CxvActionResult val = ((CxvViewModel)this).InvokeAction("Top.appModelV8.Custom Actions", "HappNode", false, new object[1] { text });
					if (val.Succeeded)
					{
						if (val.Result is IHNode iHNode)
						{
							dynamic val2 = iHNode.get_AttributeValue((short)58, Type.Missing);
							if (val2 == null || val2 == 0)
							{
								return false;
							}
						}
						continue;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool HasResults
		{
			get
			{
				if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
				{
					return false;
				}
				bool flag = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").EndsWith(".Reactions.Reactions");
				foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
				{
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main." + selectedId, "type"));
					if (flag && !text.Equals("GENERAL", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) && !text.Equals("POWERLAW", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (text.Equals("Hierarchy", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						return false;
					}
					int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main." + selectedId, "status_code"));
					if (num.HasValue)
					{
						int value = num.Value;
						if ((value & 1) != 0 || (value & 4) != 0 || (value & 8) != 0 || (value & 0x10) != 0 || (value & 0x20) != 0)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ClearVisibility

				case "Binary Interaction":
				case "Electrolyte Pair":
				case "UNIFAC Groups Binary":
				case "Streams":

					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
			}
		}

				if (text2 == "Controllers" && GetIsChildArray())
				{
					return false;
				}
				return HasTypeFieldObjects.Contains(text2);
			}
		}

					return false;

					OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged();
				}
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CanDelete");
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CanRename");
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CanCopy");

			}
		}

		private void cmdAdd_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = default(string);
			string text3 = default(string);
			bool? flag = ((MMViewModel)this).ShowAddChildDialog(text, ref text2, ref text3, false, true);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				if (text3 == "Hierarchy")
				{
					((MMViewModel)this).EOScopeChanged("Add", "", text2);
				}
				string navigatePath = ".!" + text2;
				object view = ((MMViewModel)this).View;
				((DispatcherObject)((view is MMTabPage) ? view : null)).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
				{
					GoToNodeEx(navigatePath);
				});
			}
			UpdateButtonStatus();
		}

		private void cmdEdit_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string current = enumerator.Current;
				string path = ".!" + current;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private bool GoToNodeEx(string path)
		{
			string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
			text = ((!text.Contains("Blocks")) ? text.Replace("Top.appModelV8.", "") : text.Replace("Data.", "").Substring(text.IndexOf("Blocks")));
			if (text.EndsWith("Design-Spec"))
			{
				return ((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Design-Spec", ApwnResources.BrowserDesignSpec), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			if (text.EndsWith("Model Analysis Tools.Sensitivity"))
			{
				return ((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode($"Simulation!{text}.{path.Substring(2)}", (string)null, false);
			}
			if (text.EndsWith("Data-Set"))
			{
				return ((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Data-Fit", ApwnResources.BrowserDataFit).Replace("Data-Set", ApwnResources.BrowserDataSet), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			if (text.EndsWith("Generation"))
			{
				return ((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode($"Properties!{text}.{path.Substring(2)}", (string)null, false);
			}
			if (text.EndsWith("Model Analysis Tools.Data-Fit"))
			{
				return ((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Data-Fit", ApwnResources.BrowserDataFit), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			if (text.EndsWith("Parameters.Pure Components"))
			{
				return ((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Properties!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Pure Components", ApwnResources.BrowserPureComponents), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			return ((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(path, (string)null, false);
		}

		private bool cmdDelete_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = parameter as string;
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				enumerable = new Collection<string> { text };
			}
			else
			{
				enumerable = GetSelectedIds();
				if (enumerable == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").EndsWith("Units-Sets"))
			{
				using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = enumerable.GetEnumerator();
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					switch (enumerator.Current)
					{
					case "ENG":
					case "MET":
					case "SI":
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			string text2 = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
			try
			{
				if (((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text2, "action_status", "DeleteChild")).CanInvoke)
				{
					return true;
				}
				foreach (string item in enumerable)
				{
					if (!(CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main." + item), "can_delete", (string)null)) ?? false))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			Collection<string> collection2 = new Collection<string>();
			string text = parameter as string;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				collection.Add(text);
				collection2.Add(GetBlockType(text));
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
				{
					collection.Add(selectedId);
					collection2.Add(GetBlockType(selectedId));
				}
			}
			string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			Dictionary<string, int> calculatorIdSheetDictionary = GetCalculatorIdSheetDictionary(text2, collection);
			IList<string> list = ((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren(text2, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
			if (calculatorIdSheetDictionary.Count > 0)
			{
				DeleteCalculatorSpreadsheet(calculatorIdSheetDictionary, list);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				if (collection2[collection.IndexOf(list[i])] == "Hierarchy")
				{
					((MMViewModel)this).EOScopeChanged("Delete", list[i], "");
				}
			}
			int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main", "child_count"));
			if (num.HasValue && num.Value == 0)
			{
				AllSelectedRecordsCount = 0;
				if (SelectedRowIndexes != null)
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
				}
				else
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>();
				}
			}
			UpdateButtonStatus();
		}

		private Dictionary<string, int> GetCalculatorIdSheetDictionary(string mainPath, IEnumerable<string> childrenToDelete)
		{
			Dictionary<string, int> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, int>();
			if (mainPath.Contains(".Flowsheeting Options.Calculator"))
			{
				foreach (string item in childrenToDelete)
				{
					int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main." + item + ".Input.SHEET", "value"));
					if (num.HasValue)
					{
						dictionary[item] = num.Value;
					}
				}
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		private void DeleteCalculatorSpreadsheet(Dictionary<string, int> idSheetDic, IList<string> results)
		{
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0063: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0076: Expected O, but got Unknown
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			foreach (string result in results)
			{
				if (idSheetDic.ContainsKey(result))
				{
					list.Add(idSheetDic[result]);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				((CompositePresentationEvent<ModifySpreadsheetDataSourceEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((CxvViewModel)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<ModifySpreadsheetDataSourceEvent>()).Publish(new ModifySpreadsheetDataSourceEventArgs
				{
					SheetHandles = list,
					ActionType = (SpreadsheetActionEnum)0
				});
			}
		}

		private void cmdCopy_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).CopyChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection);
			OnCanPasteChanged();
		}

		private void cmdRename_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = "";
			bool? flag = null;
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				text2 = selectedId;
				flag = ((MMViewModel)this).ShowRenameChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
				if (GetBlockType(text2) == "Hierarchy")
				{
					((MMViewModel)this).EOScopeChanged("ReName", selectedId, text2);
				}
			}
			if (flag ?? false)
			{
				SetItemFocus(text2);
			}
		}

		private void cmdHide_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).HideNodeChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
			UpdateButtonStatus();
		}

		private void cmdReveal_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).ShowRevealNodeDialog(text);
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider != null)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache();
			}
			RefreshHideRevealState();
			UpdateButtonStatus();
		}

		private void OnCanPasteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanPasteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanPasteChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CanPaste");
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).DoPaste(text);
			((MMViewModel)this).EOScopeChanged("Paste", "", "");
			UpdateButtonStatus();
		}

		private void grid_RowDoubleClickCommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string path = ".!" + enumerator.Current;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void cmdExport_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main." + selectedId);
				CxvActionResult val = ((CxvViewModel)this).InvokeAction("Top.appModelV8.Custom Actions", "HappNode", false, new object[1] { text });
				if (val.Succeeded && val.Result is IHNode iHNode)
				{
					iHNode.Export();
				}
			}
		}

		private void cmdViewResults_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (!enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				return;
			}
			string current = enumerator.Current;
			string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
			if (text.EndsWith(".Analysis"))
			{
				string text2 = ".!" + current + ".Input";
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text2, "Results", false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith("Pumparounds") || text.EndsWith(".Condenser Hcurves") || text.EndsWith(".Reboiler Hcurves"))
			{
				string text3 = ".!" + current;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text3, "Results", false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith(".Pres-Relief"))
			{
				string text4 = ".!" + current + ".Steady-State Results";
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text4, (string)null, false);
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith(".Pack Sizing") || text.EndsWith(".Pack Rating") || text.EndsWith(".Tray Sizing") || text.EndsWith(".Tray Rating") || text.EndsWith(".Packing Sizing") || text.EndsWith(".Packing Rating"))
			{
				string path = ".!" + current + ".Results";
				if (!GoToNodeEx(path))
				{
					path = ".!" + current;
					((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(path, "Results", false);
				}
			}
			else if (text.EndsWith(".Sensors"))
			{
				string text5 = ".!" + current + ".Setup";
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text5, "Results", false);
			}
			else
			{
				string path2 = ".!" + current + ".Results";
				if (GetSelectedType().Equals("HeatX", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					path2 = ".!" + current + ".Thermal Results";
				}
				GoToNodeEx(path2);
			}
		}

		private void cmdReconcile_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main." + selectedId);
				((CxvViewModel)this).Reconcile(text);
			}
			((MMViewModel)this).UpdateDisplay((object)null);
			((MMViewModel)this).UpdateAll();
		}

		private void cmdImport_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			((MMViewModel)this).Import("@main");
		}

		private void cmdAssay_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			((MMViewModel)this).ImportAssay();
		}

		private void cmdFindProperty_CommandExecute(object obj)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				string text = $"@main.{selectedId}.Input.SHOWDLG";
				int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text, "value"));
				if (num.HasValue && num.Value == 0)
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet(text, "value", (object)1);
				}
				else
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet(text, "value", (object)0);
				}
			}
		}

		private void cmdClear_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).ClearChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection);
			object view = ((MMViewModel)this).View;
			((DispatcherObject)((view is MMTabPage) ? view : null))?.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Loaded, (Action)delegate
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).UpdateDisplay((object)null);
				((MMViewModel)this).UpdateAll();
			});
		}

		private void cmdActivate_CommandExexuted(object args)
		{
			ActivateOrDeactivateObject(doActivate: true);
		}

		private bool cmdActivate_CommandCanExecute(object args)
		{
			object isActiveFieldUsed = IsActiveFieldUsed;
			CxvLogicalPropertyData val = (CxvLogicalPropertyData)((isActiveFieldUsed is CxvLogicalPropertyData) ? isActiveFieldUsed : null);
			if (val == null || !val.Value)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IEnumerable<string> selectedIds = GetSelectedIds();
			if (selectedIds != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in selectedIds)
				{
					if (!GetIsBlockActive(item))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool GetIsBlockActive(string id)
		{
			int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main." + id, "status_code"));
			if (num.HasValue && (num.Value & 0x2000) != 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ActivateOrDeactivateObject(bool doActivate)
		{
			IEnumerable<string> selectedIds = GetSelectedIds();
			if (selectedIds == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string item in selectedIds)
			{
				if (GetIsBlockActive(item) != doActivate)
				{
					((MMViewModel)this).ActivateOrDeactivateBlockNode(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main." + item), doActivate);
				}
			}
		}

		private void cmdDeactivate_CommandExexuted(object args)
		{
			ActivateOrDeactivateObject(doActivate: false);
		}

		private bool cmdDeactivate_CommandCanExecute(object args)
		{
			object isActiveFieldUsed = IsActiveFieldUsed;
			CxvLogicalPropertyData val = (CxvLogicalPropertyData)((isActiveFieldUsed is CxvLogicalPropertyData) ? isActiveFieldUsed : null);
			if (val == null || !val.Value)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IEnumerable<string> selectedIds = GetSelectedIds();
			if (selectedIds != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in selectedIds)
				{
					if (GetIsBlockActive(item))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool cmdEnable_CommandCanExecute(object args)
		{
			return false;
		}

		private void cmdEnable_CommandExecuted(object args)
		{
		}

		private bool cmdDisable_CommandCanExecute(object args)
		{
			IList list = grdChild_GridData as IList;
			List<int> list2 = new List<int>();
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null)
			{
				foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
				{
					list2.Add(selectedRowIndex);
				}
			}
			foreach (int item in list2)
			{
				if (item >= 0 && item < list.Count && list[item] is grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate && grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.CanBlockHide(grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId()))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void cmdDisable_CommandExecuted(object objs)
		{
			IEnumerable<string> selectedIds = GetSelectedIds();
			if (selectedIds == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string item in selectedIds)
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).HideNodeChildren(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main"), (IEnumerable<string>)new Collection<string> { item }, true);
			}
		}

		private void OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged()
		{
			if (this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged != null)
			{
				this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("AllSelectedRecordsCount");
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetSelectedIds()
		{
			IList gridData = grdChild_GridData as IList;
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null)
			{
				foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
				{
					list.Add(selectedRowIndex);
				}
			}
			foreach (int item in list)
			{
				if (item >= 0 && item < gridData.Count)
				{
					grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate = gridData[item] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate;
					yield return grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId();
				}
			}
		}

		private string GetSelectedType()
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
			{
				result = ((grdChild_GridData as IList)[SelectedRowIndexes[0]] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate).GetItemType();
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void RefreshHideRevealState()
		{
			Collection<string> collection = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main", "child_ids"));
			if (collection == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string item in collection)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_Enabled." + item, (object)false);
			}
		}

		private bool GetIsChildArray()
		{
			Collection<string> collection = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main", "child_ids"));
			if (collection != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in collection)
				{
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main." + item, "type"));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text == "Array")
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
			if (!((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				OnCanPasteChanged();
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("HasResults");
				return;
			}
			string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
			int num = text.LastIndexOf(".");
			string text2 = text.Substring(num + 1);
			int num2 = text.IndexOf("Subobject");
			int num3 = text.LastIndexOf("Subobject");
			string text3 = string.Empty;
			if (num2 != num3)
			{
				string text4 = text.Substring(0, num3 - 1);
				num = text4.LastIndexOf(".");
				text3 = text4.Substring(num + 1);
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_subname", (object)text3);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_type", (object)text2);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_IsActiveFieldUsed", (object)CanActivateOrDeactivateBlockTypes.Contains(text2));
			bool flag = false;
			if (HasHideFieldObjects.Contains(text2))
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_IsHideFieldUsed", (object)flag);
			RefreshHideRevealState();
		}

		private void SetItemFocus(string itemId)
		{
			int allSelectedRecordsCount = AllSelectedRecordsCount;
			if (allSelectedRecordsCount == 0 || allSelectedRecordsCount > 1 || !(grdChild_GridData is IList list))
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				string id = (list[i] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate).GetId();
				if (string.Compare(itemId, id, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>(new int[1] { i });
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private string GetBlockType(string block)
		{
			foreach (object item in grdChild_GridData as IList)
			{
				grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate = item as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate;
				if (grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId() == block)
				{
					object txtType_VarCol = grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.txtType_VarCol1;
					CxvManualVariableData val = (CxvManualVariableData)((txtType_VarCol is CxvManualVariableData) ? txtType_VarCol : null);
					if (val != null)
					{
						return val.Value as string;
					}
					object txtType_VarCol2 = grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.txtType_VarCol1;
					CxvVariableData val2 = (CxvVariableData)((txtType_VarCol2 is CxvVariableData) ? txtType_VarCol2 : null);
					if (val2 != null)
					{
						return val2.Value as string;
					}
					return null;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void SetSelectedRowFromScript(int row)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes == null)
			{
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>();
			}
			SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
			SelectedRowIndexes.Add(row);
			AllSelectedRecordsCount = 1;
		}

		private void UpdateButtonStatus()
		{
			if (((MMViewModel)this).View is MMGeneric_List2D mMGeneric_List2D)
			{
				mMGeneric_List2D.RefreshSelection();
			}
		}

	public class StatusConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			return (value as string) switch
			{
				"Inactive" => GridFormsResources.MMGeneric_List2D_Status_Inactive, 
				"Active" => GridFormsResources.MMGeneric_List2D_Status_Active, 
				_ => value.ToString(), 
			};
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

		private void grdChild_CanPasteChecking(object sender, MMScrollAreaCanPasteCheckingEventArgs e)
		{
			ObservableCollection<int> selectedFieldIds = grdChild.SelectedFieldIds;
			if (selectedFieldIds != null && selectedFieldIds.Count == 1 && selectedFieldIds[0] == 0 && e.PastingData != null && e.PastingData.Count > 0 && !MMScrollAreaMS.ValidName(e.PastingData[0][0]))
			{
				e.CanPaste = false;
				((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e).Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private (bool Handled, string DisplayText) ConvertDisplayTextInTable(IDataCell dc, string text)
		{
			if (dc.Column.Index == 1)
			{
				if (string.Equals(text, "material", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return (Handled: true, DisplayText: Resources.rectype_streams_material);
				}
				if (string.Equals(text, "work", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return (Handled: true, DisplayText: Resources.rectype_streams_work);
				}
				if (string.Equals(text, "heat", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return (Handled: true, DisplayText: Resources.rectype_streams_heat);
				}
			}
			return (Handled: false, DisplayText: text);
		}

	public class MMGeneric_Results_Status_ViewModel : MMTabPageViewModel
	{
		public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration var_Convergence_Property;

		public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration var_Properties_Property;

		private string _header;

		private ICommand _linkCommand;

		public object var_Convergence => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(var_Convergence_Property);

		public object var_Properties => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(var_Properties_Property);

		public object var_Message => (object)new CxvDescriptionPropertyDataV2((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, new CxvDescriptionProperty
		{
			DataPath = "@smain.Output.PER_ERROR",
			IsReadOnly = true,
			IsCalculated = true
		});

		public string Header
		{
			get
			{
				if (((MMViewModel)this).GetProductCode() == 1)
				{
					_header = Resources.MMGeneric_Results_Status_ViewModel__Header_AspenPlusMessages;
				}
				if (((MMViewModel)this).GetProductCode() == 2)
				{
					_header = Resources.MMGeneric_Results_Status_ViewModel__Header_AspenPropertiesMessages;
				}
				return _header;
			}
		}

		public ICommand LinkCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_linkCommand == null)
				{
					_linkCommand = new SerializableCommand(LinkCommandExecuted);
				}
				return _linkCommand;
			}
		}

		public override void Unload()
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).Unload();
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).UnityContainer != null)
			{
				((CompositePresentationEvent<UpdateDisplayEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((CxvViewModel)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateDisplayEvent>()).Unsubscribe((Action<UpdateDisplayEventArgs>)OnUpdateDisplayEvent);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				if (((CxvViewModel)this).UnityContainer != null)
				{
					((CompositePresentationEvent<UpdateDisplayEventArgs>)(object)UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(((CxvViewModel)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateDisplayEvent>()).Subscribe((Action<UpdateDisplayEventArgs>)OnUpdateDisplayEvent);
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("extra_message_path"))))
				{
					if (CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main")) == "Top.appModelV8.Results Summary")
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("extra_message_path", (object)"Run-Status");
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("smain", (object)((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.@extra_message_path"));
					}
					else
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("smain", ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main"));
					}
				}
				else
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("smain", (object)((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.@extra_message_path"));
				}
			}
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		private void OnUpdateDisplayEvent(UpdateDisplayEventArgs args)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("extra_message_path"))))
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("smain", ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main"));
			}
			else
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("smain", (object)((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.@extra_message_path"));
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("var_Message");
		}

		private void LinkCommandExecuted(object args)
		{
			DescriptionHyperlinkArgs val = (DescriptionHyperlinkArgs)((args is DescriptionHyperlinkArgs) ? args : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ApwnResources.BrowserResults;
			if (val.ObjectType.StartsWith("Components.PetroCharacterization"))
			{
				val.ObjectType = "Components.Petro Characterization";
			}
			else if (val.ObjectType == "Properties.Estimation")
			{
				val.ObjectType = "Estimation";
			}
			else if (val.ObjectType == "Properties.Analysis" || val.ObjectType == "Analysis")
			{
				val.ObjectType = "Analysis";
				text = ApwnResources.BrowserInput;
			}
			else if (val.ObjectType == "Properties.Regression")
			{
				val.ObjectType = "Regression";
			}
			else if (val.ObjectType == "Sensors")
			{
				text = "Setup";
			}
			else if (val.ObjectType == "Flowsheeting Options.Pres-Relief")
			{
				string text2 = "";
				if (!val.ObjectId.Contains('.'))
				{
					text2 = $"Top.appModelV8.Flowsheeting Options.Pres-Relief.{val.ObjectId}.CC Nodes.STATERESUL";
				}
				else
				{
					StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder("Top.appModelV8.Blocks.", 100);
					string[] array = val.ObjectId.Trim().Split('.');
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length - 1; i++)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(array[i]);
						stringBuilder.Append(".");
						stringBuilder.Append((i == array.Length - 2) ? val.ObjectType : "Blocks");
						stringBuilder.Append(".Data.");
					}
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat("Flosheeting Options.Pres-Relief.{0}.CC Nodes.STATERESUL", array[array.Length - 1]);
					text2 = stringBuilder.ToString();
				}
				int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text2, "value"));
				text = ((!num.HasValue || num.Value == 0) ? ApwnResources.BrowserDynamicResults : ApwnResources.BrowserSteadyStateResults);
			}
			if (val.RecordType == "HeatX")
			{
				text = "Thermal Results";
			}
			if (!val.ObjectId.Contains('.'))
			{
				if (val.RecordType != "Convergence.Batch-Options")
				{
					((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode($"{val.ObjectType}.{val.ObjectId}.{text}", ApwnResources.StatusTabHeader, true);
				}
				else
				{
					((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode("Convergence.Batch-Options.Results", (string)null, true);
				}
				return;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder("Simulation!Blocks.", 100);
			string[] array2 = val.ObjectId.Trim().Split('.');
			for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length - 1; j++)
			{
				stringBuilder2.Append(array2[j]);
				stringBuilder2.Append(".");
				stringBuilder2.Append((j == array2.Length - 2) ? val.ObjectType : "Blocks");
				stringBuilder2.Append(".");
			}
			if (val.RecordType != "Convergence.Batch-Options")
			{
				stringBuilder2.Append(array2[array2.Length - 1]);
				stringBuilder2.Append(".");
				stringBuilder2.Append(text);
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(stringBuilder2.ToString(), ApwnResources.StatusTabHeader, true);
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder2.Append("Results");
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(stringBuilder2.ToString(), (string)null, true);
			}
		}

	public class SerializableCommand : ICommand
	{
		private readonly Action<object> _executeMethod;

		private readonly Predicate<object> _canExecuteMethod;

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged;

		public SerializableCommand(Action<object> executeMethod)
			: this(executeMethod, null)
		{
		}

		public SerializableCommand(Action<object> executeMethod, Predicate<object> canExecuteMethod)
		{
			_executeMethod = executeMethod;
			_canExecuteMethod = canExecuteMethod;
		}

		public bool CanExecute(object args)
		{
			if (_canExecuteMethod != null)
			{
				return _canExecuteMethod(args);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void OnCanExecuteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanExecuteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanExecuteChanged(this, null);
			}
		}

		public void Execute(object args)
		{
			if (_executeMethod != null)
			{
				_executeMethod(args);
			}
		}
	}

			{

				{
					//IL_0060: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
					//IL_0065: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
					//IL_006c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

					//IL_0080: Expected O, but got Unknown
					object value = ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(txtType_VarCol1_Property);
					CxvVariableData val = (CxvVariableData)((value is CxvVariableData) ? value : null);
					if (val != null && val.Value != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(val.Value.ToString()))
					{
						string localizedCol = GetLocalizedCol1("rectype_" + RecType + "_" + val.Value.ToString(), val.Value.ToString());
						return (object)new CxvManualVariableData
						{
							Value = localizedCol,
							IsReadOnly = true,
							IsCalculated = false
						};
					}
					return val;
				}
			}
