			private string GetLocalizedCol1(string key, string defaultOptionValue)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
				{
					string name = Regex.Replace(key, "[^a-zA-Z0-9]", "_").ToLower();
					text = ApwnRecTypeResource.ResourceManager.GetObject(name, ApwnRecTypeResource.Culture) as string;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return text;
				}
				return defaultOptionValue;
			}

			protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)

				{
					object obj = txtName_var1;
					CxvPropertyBase property = ((CxvPropertyData)((obj is CxvCustomTooltipData) ? obj : null)).Property;

					{
						val.ObjectType = "Stream";
					}
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
			}
		}

		public bool CanPaste
		{
			get
			{
				if (!ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("Aspen Plus Selection"))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (ClipboardWrapper.ContainsData("Aspen Plus Selection Type"))
				{
					IDataObject dataObject = ClipboardWrapper.GetDataObject();
					if (dataObject != null && dataObject.GetData("Aspen Plus Selection Type", autoConvert: false) is MemoryStream stream)
					{
						string text = new StreamReader(stream).ReadToEnd();
						string text2 = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
						try
						{
							return CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text2, "action_status", "CanPaste:" + text)).CanInvoke;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public CxvLogicalPropertyData cmdPaste_CommandCanExecute
		{
			get
			{
				object value = ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(cmdPaste_CommandCanExecuteProperty);
				return (CxvLogicalPropertyData)((value is CxvLogicalPropertyData) ? value : null);
			}
		}

		public int AllSelectedRecordsCount
		{
			get
			{
				return _allSelectedRecordsCount;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _allSelectedRecordsCount)
				{
					_allSelectedRecordsCount = value;
					OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CanPasteChanged;

		public event EventHandler AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged;

		private void cmdAdd_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = null;
			bool? flag = ((MMViewModel)this).ShowAddChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				string text3 = ".!" + text2;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text3, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdEdit_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string current = enumerator.Current;
				string text = ".!" + current;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdClear_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).ClearChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection);
		}

		private void cmdCopy_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).CopyChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection);
			OnCanPasteChanged();
		}

		private void cmdRename_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = "";
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				text2 = selectedId;
				((MMViewModel)this).ShowRenameChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdHide_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).HideNodeChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
		}

		private void cmdReveal_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).ShowRevealNodeDialog(text);
		}

		private void OnCanPasteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanPasteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanPasteChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CanPaste");
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).DoPaste(text);
		}

		private void grid_RowDoubleClickCommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string text = ".!" + enumerator.Current;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged()
		{
			if (this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged != null)
			{
				this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("AllSelectedRecordsCount");
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetSelectedIds()
		{
			IList gridData = grdChild_GridData as IList;
			foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
			{
				grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate = gridData[selectedRowIndex] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate;
				yield return grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId();
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedRowFromScript(int row)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes == null)
			{
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>();
			}
			SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
			SelectedRowIndexes.Add(row);
			AllSelectedRecordsCount = 1;
		}

		private void rowDoubleClick_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string text = ".!" + enumerator.Current;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdAdd_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0005: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			new ShowAddChildDialogEventArgs().ParentPath = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = null;
			bool? flag = ((MMViewModel)this).ShowAddChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				string text3 = ".!" + text2;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text3, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdEdit_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			int allSelectedRecordsCount = AllSelectedRecordsCount;
			if (allSelectedRecordsCount == 0 || allSelectedRecordsCount > 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string current = enumerator.Current;
				string text = ".!" + current;
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(text, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
		}

		private void cmdRename_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			int allSelectedRecordsCount = AllSelectedRecordsCount;
			if (allSelectedRecordsCount == 0 || allSelectedRecordsCount > 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = "";
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				text2 = selectedId;
				((MMViewModel)this).ShowRenameChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdHide_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).HideNodeChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
		}

		private void cmdReveal_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).ShowRevealNodeDialog(text);
		}

		private void OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged()
		{
			if (this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged != null)
			{
				this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("AllSelectedRecordsCount");
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetSelectedIds()
		{
			IList gridData = grdChild_GridData as IList;
			foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
			{
				grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate = gridData[selectedRowIndex] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate;
				yield return grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
			_ = ((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate;
		}

		public void SetSelectedRowFromScript(int row)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes == null)
			{
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>();
			}
			SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
			SelectedRowIndexes.Add(row);
			AllSelectedRecordsCount = 1;
		}

		private void GoToNodeEx(string path)
		{
			string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
			text = ((!text.Contains("Blocks")) ? text.Replace("Top.appModelV8.", "") : text.Replace("Data.", "").Substring(text.IndexOf("Blocks")));
			if (_TPSARHost == TPSSARHostEnum.RadFracTPSAR && _TPSARType == TPSARTypeEnum.TraySizing)
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(string.Format("Simulation!{0}.{1}", text.Replace("Tray Sizing", ApwnResources.BrowserTraySizing).Replace("Subobjects", ApwnResources.BrowserSizingAndRating), path.Substring(2)), (string)null, false);
			}
			else
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).GoToNode(path, (string)null, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdAdd_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0005: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			new ShowAddChildDialogEventArgs().ParentPath = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = null;
			bool? flag = ((MMViewModel)this).ShowAddChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				string path = ".!" + text2;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void cmdEdit_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AllSelectedRecordsCount != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string current = enumerator.Current;
				string path = ".!" + current;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
			int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main", "child_count"));
			if (num.HasValue && num.Value == 0)
			{
				AllSelectedRecordsCount = 0;
				if (SelectedRowIndexes != null)
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
				}
				else
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>();
				}
			}
		}

		private void cmdCopy_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0035: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_004d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0068: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (_TPSARType == TPSARTypeEnum.NotTPSAR || _TPSARHost == TPSSARHostEnum.Unknown)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
				{
					Message = Resources.MMGeneric_Subobj_ViewModel__MSGBox_Message1,
					Title = Resources.MMGeneric_Subobj_ViewModel__MSGBox_Title_AspenPlus,
					MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
					MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Hand
				});
			}
			else if (AllSelectedRecordsCount < 1)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
				{
					Message = Resources.MMGeneric_Subobj_ViewModel__MSGBox_Message2,
					Title = Resources.MMGeneric_Subobj_ViewModel__MSGBox_Title_AspenPlus,
					MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
					MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Asterisk
				});
			}
			else
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
				MmgExtensions.CopyTPSARSections((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, GetSelectedIds());
				OnCanPasteChanged();
			}
		}

		private void cmdRename_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			int allSelectedRecordsCount = AllSelectedRecordsCount;
			if (allSelectedRecordsCount == 0 || allSelectedRecordsCount > 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				list.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text2 = "";
			foreach (string item in list)
			{
				text2 = item;
				((MMViewModel)this).ShowRenameChildDialog(text, ref text2, false);
			}
		}

		private void cmdHide_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string selectedId in GetSelectedIds())
			{
				collection.Add(selectedId);
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).HideNodeChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)collection, false);
		}

		private void cmdReveal_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			((MMViewModel)this).ShowRevealNodeDialog(text);
		}

		private bool ClipboardHasTPSAR()
		{
			if (!ClipboardWrapper.ContainsText(TextDataFormat.Text))
			{
				return false;
			}
			try
			{
				string text = ClipboardWrapper.GetText(TextDataFormat.Text);
				XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
				xmlDocument.LoadXml(text);
				XmlElement documentElement = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
				if (documentElement != null && documentElement.Name == "APlusTPSAR")
				{
					string attribute = documentElement.GetAttribute("TPSARType");
					if (attribute != null)
					{
						switch (_TPSARType)
						{
						case TPSARTypeEnum.PackRating:
							if (attribute == "Pack Rating" || attribute == "PackRating")
							{
								return true;
							}
							break;
						case TPSARTypeEnum.PackSizing:
							if (attribute == "Pack Sizing" || attribute == "PackSizing")
							{
								return true;
							}
							break;
						case TPSARTypeEnum.TrayRating:
							if (attribute == "Tray Rating" || attribute == "TrayRating")
							{
								return true;
							}
							break;
						case TPSARTypeEnum.TraySizing:
							if (attribute == "Tray Sizing" || attribute == "TraySizing")
							{
								return true;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnCanPasteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanPasteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanPasteChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CanPaste");
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0035: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (_TPSARType == TPSARTypeEnum.NotTPSAR || _TPSARHost == TPSSARHostEnum.Unknown)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
				{
					Message = Resources.MMGeneric_Subobj_ViewModel__MSGBox_Message3,
					Title = Resources.MMGeneric_Subobj_ViewModel__MSGBox_Title_AspenPlus,
					MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
					MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Hand
				});
			}
			else
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
				MmgExtensions.PasteTPSARSections((MMViewModel)(object)this, text);
			}
		}

		private void grid_RowDoubleClickCommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetSelectedIds().GetEnumerator();
			if (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string path = ".!" + enumerator.Current;
				GoToNodeEx(path);
			}
		}

		private void OnAllSelectedRecordsCountChanged()

				this.AllSelectedRecordsCountChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("AllSelectedRecordsCount");

		private void GetTPSARInfo()
		{
			_TPSARHost = TPSSARHostEnum.Unknown;
			_TPSARType = TPSARTypeEnum.NotTPSAR;
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			string text2 = CxsPath.ParentPath(CxsPath.ParentPath(text));
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				return;
			}
			empty = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text2, "type", (string)null));
			switch (empty)
			{
			case "RadFrac":
				_TPSARHost = TPSSARHostEnum.RadFracTPSAR;
				break;
			case "PetroFrac":
				_TPSARHost = TPSSARHostEnum.PetroFracMainTPSAR;
				break;
			case "MultiFrac":
				_TPSARHost = TPSSARHostEnum.MultiFracTPSAR;
				break;
			default:
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty) || text2.Contains("Subobjects.Strippers"))
				{
					string text3 = CxsPath.ParentPath(text2);
					if (CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text3, "id", (string)null)) == "Strippers")
					{
						_TPSARHost = TPSSARHostEnum.PetroFracStripperTPSAR;
						_StripperName = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text2, "id", (string)null));
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			switch (CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, "id", (string)null)))
			{
			case "Tray Rating":
				_TPSARType = TPSARTypeEnum.TrayRating;
				break;
			case "Pack Rating":
				_TPSARType = TPSARTypeEnum.PackRating;
				break;
			case "Tray Sizing":
				_TPSARType = TPSARTypeEnum.TraySizing;
				break;
			case "Pack Sizing":
				_TPSARType = TPSARTypeEnum.PackSizing;
				break;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				GetTPSARInfo();
				if (_TPSARType == TPSARTypeEnum.NotTPSAR)
				{
					PasteButtonVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					CopyButtonVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				else
				{
					PasteButtonVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
					CopyButtonVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPasteButtonVisibilityChanged()
		{
			if (this.PasteButtonVisibilityChanged != null)
			{
				this.PasteButtonVisibilityChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("PasteButtonVisibility");
		}

		protected virtual void OnCopyButtonVisibilityChanged()
		{

			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("CopyButtonVisibility");
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> GetSelectedIds()
		{
			IList gridData = grdChild_GridData;
			foreach (int selectedRowIndex in SelectedRowIndexes)
			{
				grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate = gridData[selectedRowIndex] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate;
				yield return grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate.GetId();
			}
		}

		private void SetItemFocus(string itemId)
		{
			int allSelectedRecordsCount = AllSelectedRecordsCount;
			if (allSelectedRecordsCount == 0 || allSelectedRecordsCount > 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
			IList list = grdChild_GridData;
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				string id = (list[i] as grdChild_GridData_ItemTemplate).GetId();
				if (string.Compare(itemId, id, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes.Add(i);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedRowFromScript(int row)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes == null)
			{
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>();
			}
			SelectedRowIndexes.Clear();
			SelectedRowIndexes.Add(row);
			AllSelectedRecordsCount = 1;
		}

	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values == null || values.Length < 3)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (!((values[1] as bool?) ?? true) || !((values[2] as bool?) ?? true))
			{
				return values[0] as string;
			}
			return null;
		}
